function infAbrirVenue(cat){
  const v=INF_VENUES[cat];const t=INF_TIPOS[v.tipo];
  // Tenta carregar via GPS primeiro; fallback para lista fixa
  infAbrirVenueSheet(cat, v.itens);
  if(navigator.geolocation){
    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(pos=>{
      infCarregarVenuesGPS(cat, pos.coords.latitude, pos.coords.longitude);
    }, null, {timeout:5000, maximumAge:30000});
  }
}
async function infCarregarVenuesGPS(cat, lat, lon){
  const queries = {
    bares: `node(around:800,${lat},${lon})[amenity~"^(bar|pub|restaurant|cafe|nightclub)$"][name];`,
    hoteis:`node(around:2000,${lat},${lon})[tourism~"^(hotel|hostel|motel|guest_house)$"][name];`,
    eventos:`(node(around:5000,${lat},${lon})[amenity~"^(theatre|cinema|arts_centre)$"][name];node(around:5000,${lat},${lon})[leisure="stadium"][name];);`,
    turistico:`node(around:3000,${lat},${lon})[tourism~"^(museum|attraction|viewpoint|gallery)$"][name];`,
  };
  const oq = queries[cat]; if(!oq) return;
  try{
    const r=await fetch(`https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter?data=[out:json];${encodeURIComponent(oq)}out 20;`);
    const d=await r.json();
    if(!d.elements||!d.elements.length) return;
    // Calcula distância e ordena
    const R=6371;
    const dist=(a,b,c,dd)=>R*2*Math.asin(Math.sqrt(Math.sin(((c-a)*Math.PI/180)/2)**2+Math.cos(a*Math.PI/180)*Math.cos(c*Math.PI/180)*Math.sin(((dd-b)*Math.PI/180)/2)**2));
    const itens=d.elements
      .filter(e=>e.tags&&e.tags.name)
      .map(e=>({n:e.tags.name, sub:e.tags['addr:suburb']||e.tags['addr:city']||'Via GPS', km:dist(lat,lon,e.lat||lat,e.lon||lon)}))
      .sort((a,b)=>a.km-b.km)
      .slice(0,12)
      .map(e=>({...e,distStr:(e.km<1?Math.round(e.km*1000)+'m':e.km.toFixed(1)+'km')}));
    if(itens.length) infAbrirVenueSheet(cat, itens, true);
  }catch(e){console.warn('overpass venues',e);}
}
function infAbrirVenueSheet(cat, itens, viaGPS){
  const v=INF_VENUES[cat]; if(!v) return;
  const t=INF_TIPOS[v.tipo]||INF_TIPOS.bairro;
  const ic=document.getElementById('inf-venue-ic');
  if(ic){ic.style.background=t.bg;ic.innerHTML=infIconSvg(v.tipo,t.cor,17);}
  const titleEl=document.getElementById('inf-venue-title');
  if(titleEl) titleEl.textContent=v.titulo;
  const subEl=document.getElementById('inf-venue-sub');
  if(subEl) subEl.textContent=viaGPS?'Ordenado pela sua localização':'Locais populares';
  const listEl=document.getElementById('inf-venue-list');
  const displayItens=itens||v.itens||[];
  if(listEl) listEl.innerHTML=displayItens.map(item=>
    `<div class="inf-loc-item" onclick="infFecharOv('inf-ov-venue');infAbrirLocal(null,'${(item.n||'').replace(/'/g,"\'")}','${v.tipo}')">
      <div class="inf-loc-ic" style="background:${t.bg}">${infIconSvg(v.tipo,t.cor,15)}</div>
      <div class="inf-loc-body"><div class="inf-loc-name">${item.n||''}</div><div class="inf-loc-sub">${item.sub||item.distStr||''}</div></div>
    </div>`
  ).join('');
  const ovEl=document.getElementById('inf-ov-venue');
  if(ovEl) ovEl.classList.add('open');
}

  let html=`<div class="inf-ropts show" id="inf-ropts-main"><div class="inf-ropt-lbl">${INF_PLBL[pid]}</div>`;
  if(p.opts){html+=p.opts.map(o=>`<button class="inf-ropt" onclick="infSelOpt(this,'${o.v}','${o.c||''}')">${o.v}</button>`).join('');}
  else if(p.sub){html+=p.sub.map(o=>`<button class="inf-ropt" onclick="infSelFisc(this,'${o.v}')">${o.v}</button>`).join('');}
  html+='</div>';
  if(pid==='fiscalizacao')html+=`<div class="inf-ropts" id="inf-ropts-fisc-acao"><div class="inf-ropt-lbl">O que estão fazendo?</div>${p.subacao.map(o=>`<button class="inf-ropt" onclick="infSelOpt(this,'${o.v}','${o.c||''}')">${o.v}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
  document.getElementById('inf-rep-opcoes-wrap').innerHTML=html;
}

function infBuscarGlobal(q){
  const dd=document.getElementById('inf-search-dd');
  if(!q.trim()){dd.classList.remove('show');return;}
  const qL=q.toLowerCase();
  let res=INF_SDBDB.filter(x=>x.n.toLowerCase().includes(qL));
  for(const [,v] of Object.entries(INF_VENUES)){
    for(const item of (v.itens||[])){
      if(item.n.toLowerCase().includes(qL)||(item.sub&&item.sub.toLowerCase().includes(qL))){
        res.push({n:item.n+(item.sub?' ('+item.sub+')':''),t:v.tipo||'bairro'});
      }
    }
  }
  const _seen=new Set(); res=res.filter(x=>{if(_seen.has(x.n))return false;_seen.add(x.n);return true;}).slice(0,8);
  dd.innerHTML=res.length?res.map(x=>{const t=INF_TIPOS[x.t]||INF_TIPOS.bairro;return`<div class="inf-sd-item" onclick="infToggleSearch();infAbrirLocal(null,'${x.n}','${x.t}')"><div class="inf-sd-ic" style="background:${t.bg}">${infIconSvg(x.t,t.cor,14)}</div><div><div class="inf-sd-name">${x.n}</div><div class="inf-sd-sub">${t.label}</div></div></div>`;}).join(''):'<div class="inf-sd-empty">Nenhum resultado</div>';
  dd.classList.add('show');
}

(function infWatchTopbarHeight(){
  const el = document.getElementById('inf-topbar');
  if (!el || typeof ResizeObserver === 'undefined') return;
  const apply = () => document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--inf-topbar-h', el.offsetHeight + 'px');
  new ResizeObserver(apply).observe(el);
  apply();
})();

function infCidadeItemHtml(c){
  const isActive = c.name === _infCity.name && c.uf === _infCity.uf;
  return `<div class="inf-city-item ${isActive?'active':''}" onclick="infEscolherCidade('${c.name.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${c.uf}')">
    <div class="inf-city-item-ic"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/><path d="M12 2v3M12 19v3M2 12h3M19 12h3"/></svg></div>
    <div><div class="inf-city-item-name">${c.name}</div><div class="inf-city-item-uf">${c.uf}</div></div>
    ${isActive ? '<svg class="inf-city-item-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="17" height="17" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"/></svg>' : ''}
  </div>`;

let _infCitySearchDebounce = null;
function infBuscarCidade(q){
  clearTimeout(_infCitySearchDebounce);
  if (!q || q.trim().length < 2) { infRenderCidadeDefaults(); return; }
  _infCitySearchDebounce = setTimeout(async () => {
    try {
      const r = await fetch(`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?city=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&country=Brazil&format=json&addressdetails=1&limit=8`, {headers:{'User-Agent':'MoB/1.0'}});
      const data = await r.json();
      const cidades = data
        .filter(d => d.address && (d.address.city || d.address.town || d.address.municipality))
        .map(d => ({name: d.address.city || d.address.town || d.address.municipality, uf: d.address.state_code || (d.address['ISO3166-2-lvl4']||'').split('-')[1] || ''}));
      const vistos = new Set();
      const unicos = cidades.filter(c => { const k = c.name+c.uf; if (vistos.has(k)) return false; vistos.add(k); return true; });
      document.getElementById('inf-city-list').innerHTML = unicos.length
        ? unicos.map(infCidadeItemHtml).join('')
        : '<div class="inf-empty">Nenhuma cidade encontrada</div>';
    } catch(e) { console.warn('busca cidade', e); }
  }, 400);
}

    const u=`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?format=jsonv2&limit=1&countrycodes=br&q=${encodeURIComponent(name+', '+uf+', Brasil')}`;
    const r=await fetch(u,{headers:{Accept:'application/json'}}),d=await r.json();
    if(d?.[0]){const lat=Number(d[0].lat),lon=Number(d[0].lon);_infGps={lat,lon,at:Date.now()};infRenderNearbyVenueLists(lat,lon);}
  }catch(e){}
  try{infCarregarFeedAgora?.();}catch(e){}
}

function infCityKey(name){
  const n=(name||'').trim();
  if(/^rio de janeiro$/i.test(n))return'rio';
  return n.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-').replace(/^-|-$/g,'')||null;
}

async function infReverseCidade(lat,lon){
  try{
    const u=`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/reverse?format=jsonv2&lat=${encodeURIComponent(lat)}&lon=${encodeURIComponent(lon)}&zoom=10&addressdetails=1&accept-language=pt-BR`;
    const r=await fetch(u,{headers:{Accept:'application/json'}});if(!r.ok)throw new Error('reverse');
    const d=await r.json(),a=d.address||{};
    const name=a.city||a.town||a.municipality||a.village||a.county;
    const iso=a['ISO3166-2-lvl4']||a['ISO3166-2-lvl6']||'';
    return name?{name,uf:(iso.split('-')[1]||'').toUpperCase()}:null;
  }catch(e){return null;}
}

  if(!navigator.geolocation)return;
  navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(async pos=>{
    const lat=pos.coords.latitude,lon=pos.coords.longitude;_infGps={lat,lon,at:Date.now()};

  },()=>{const el=document.getElementById('inf-city-name');if(el)el.textContent=_infCity?.name||'Escolher cidade';},{enableHighAccuracy:true,timeout:10000,maximumAge:60000});
}

async function infRenderNearbyVenueLists(lat,lon){
  const q=`[out:json][timeout:18];(`+
    `nwr(around:7000,${lat},${lon})[shop=mall][name];`+
    `nwr(around:1800,${lat},${lon})[amenity~"^(bar|pub|restaurant|cafe|nightclub)$"][name];`+
    `nwr(around:6000,${lat},${lon})[tourism~"^(hotel|hostel|motel|guest_house)$"][name];`+
    `nwr(around:10000,${lat},${lon})[amenity~"^(theatre|cinema|arts_centre|events_venue)$"][name];`+
    `nwr(around:10000,${lat},${lon})[leisure=stadium][name];`+
    `nwr(around:7000,${lat},${lon})[tourism~"^(museum|attraction|viewpoint|gallery)$"][name];`+
    `);out center 300;`;
  try{
    const r=await fetch(`https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter?data=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`);if(!r.ok)throw new Error('overpass');
    const d=await r.json(),groups={shoppings:[],bares:[],hoteis:[],eventos:[],turistico:[]};
    const R=6371,toRad=x=>x*Math.PI/180;
    const dist=(a,b,c,dd)=>{const dl=toRad(c-a),dn=toRad(dd-b),x=Math.sin(dl/2)**2+Math.cos(toRad(a))*Math.cos(toRad(c))*Math.sin(dn/2)**2;return R*2*Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(x),Math.sqrt(1-x));};
    for(const e of(d.elements||[])){
      const t=e.tags||{},n=t.name;if(!n)continue;const elat=e.lat??e.center?.lat,elon=e.lon??e.center?.lon;if(elat==null||elon==null)continue;
      let cat=null;
      if(t.shop==='mall')cat='shoppings';
      else if(/^(bar|pub|restaurant|cafe|nightclub)$/.test(t.amenity||''))cat='bares';
      else if(/^(hotel|hostel|motel|guest_house)$/.test(t.tourism||''))cat='hoteis';
      else if(/^(theatre|cinema|arts_centre|events_venue)$/.test(t.amenity||'')||t.leisure==='stadium')cat='eventos';
      else if(/^(museum|attraction|viewpoint|gallery)$/.test(t.tourism||''))cat='turistico';
      if(!cat)continue;const km=dist(lat,lon,elat,elon);
      groups[cat].push({n,sub:t['addr:suburb']||t['addr:city']||(km<1?`${Math.round(km*1000)} m`:`${km.toFixed(1)} km`),km});
    }
    for(const[cat,items0]of Object.entries(groups)){
      const items=[...new Map(items0.sort((a,b)=>a.km-b.km).map(x=>[x.n.toLowerCase(),x])).values()].slice(0,12);
      infRenderNearbyVenueTab(cat,items);
    }
    infCarregarBadgesListagem();
  }catch(e){console.warn('locais próximos',e);}
}

function infRenderNearbyVenueTab(cat,items){
  const el=document.getElementById('inf-vlist-'+cat);if(!el)return;const v=INF_VENUES[cat];if(!v)return;const t=INF_TIPOS[v.tipo]||INF_TIPOS.bairro;
  if(!items.length){el.innerHTML='<div class="inf-empty">Nenhum local próximo encontrado</div>';return;}
  el.innerHTML=items.map(item=>`<div class="inf-vitem" onclick="infAbrirLocal(this,'${item.n.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${v.tipo}')"><div class="inf-vitem-ic" style="background:${t.bg}">${infIconSvg(v.tipo,t.cor,15)}</div><div><div class="inf-vitem-name">${item.n}</div><div class="inf-vitem-sub">${item.sub||''}</div></div><div class="inf-vitem-badges" data-vname="${esc(item.n)}"></div><div class="inf-vitem-ct z">–</div></div>`).join('');
}

  document.querySelectorAll('#inf-scroll-content > .inf-panelbody').forEach(p=>p.classList.add('hide'));
  const p=document.getElementById('inf-tab-'+id);if(p)p.classList.remove('hide');
}

function infCityKey(name){
  const n=(name||'').trim();
  if(/^rio de janeiro$/i.test(n))return'rio';
  return n.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-').replace(/^-|-$/g,'')||null;
}

async function infReverseCidade(lat,lon){
  try{
    const u=`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/reverse?format=jsonv2&lat=${encodeURIComponent(lat)}&lon=${encodeURIComponent(lon)}&zoom=10&addressdetails=1&accept-language=pt-BR`;
    const r=await fetch(u,{headers:{Accept:'application/json'}});if(!r.ok)throw new Error('reverse');
    const d=await r.json(),a=d.address||{};
    const name=a.city||a.town||a.municipality||a.village||a.county;
    const iso=a['ISO3166-2-lvl4']||a['ISO3166-2-lvl6']||'';
    return name?{name,uf:(iso.split('-')[1]||'').toUpperCase()}:null;
  }catch(e){return null;}
}

let _infCitySearchDebounce = null;
function infBuscarCidade(q){
  clearTimeout(_infCitySearchDebounce);
  if (!q || q.trim().length < 2) { infRenderCidadeDefaults(); return; }
  _infCitySearchDebounce = setTimeout(async () => {
    try {
      const r = await fetch(`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?city=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&country=Brazil&format=json&addressdetails=1&limit=8`, {headers:{'User-Agent':'MoB/1.0'}});
      const data = await r.json();
      const cidades = data
        .filter(d => d.address && (d.address.city || d.address.town || d.address.municipality))
        .map(d => ({name: d.address.city || d.address.town || d.address.municipality, uf: d.address.state_code || (d.address['ISO3166-2-lvl4']||'').split('-')[1] || ''}));
      const vistos = new Set();
      const unicos = cidades.filter(c => { const k = c.name+c.uf; if (vistos.has(k)) return false; vistos.add(k); return true; });
      document.getElementById('inf-city-list').innerHTML = unicos.length
        ? unicos.map(infCidadeItemHtml).join('')
        : '<div class="inf-empty">Nenhuma cidade encontrada</div>';
    } catch(e) { console.warn('busca cidade', e); }
  }, 400);
}
async function infEscolherCidade(name, uf){
  _infCity={name,uf};localStorage.setItem('sm_inf_city',JSON.stringify(_infCity));document.getElementById('inf-city-name').textContent=name;closeMo('mo-inf-cidade');
  try{
    const u=`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?format=jsonv2&limit=1&countrycodes=br&q=${encodeURIComponent(name+', '+uf+', Brasil')}`;
    const r=await fetch(u,{headers:{Accept:'application/json'}}),d=await r.json();
    if(d?.[0]){const lat=Number(d[0].lat),lon=Number(d[0].lon);_infGps={lat,lon,at:Date.now()};infRenderNearbyVenueLists(lat,lon);}
  }catch(e){}
  try{infCarregarFeedAgora?.();}catch(e){}
}
function infToggleSearch(){
  const el = document.getElementById('inf-search-fixed');
  const btn = document.getElementById('inf-search-toggle');
  if(!el) return;
  const show = el.style.display === 'none';
  el.style.display = show ? 'flex' : 'none';
  if(btn){ btn.style.background = show ? '#0A2F6B' : ''; btn.style.color = show ? '#fff' : ''; }
  if(show){ document.getElementById('inf-search-bar')?.focus(); }
  else {
    const inp = document.getElementById('inf-search-bar'); if(inp) inp.value='';
    const dd = document.getElementById('inf-search-dd'); if(dd) dd.classList.remove('show');
  }
}
// Botão "Relatar agora" (barra fixa embaixo, Opção B aprovada) — abre o
// seletor de tipo. Reaproveita ícone/expMin do FEED_TYPES (blitz/risco/
// transito), só troca label/cor pro contexto do Informes (Policiamento/
// Alerta/Trânsito) — mesma chave de type no banco, então o composer do
// Início (que ainda usa os nomes antigos) continua compatível com o
// mesmo dado. Construído dentro da função (não como const no topo do
// arquivo) porque FEED_TYPES só existe mais abaixo — na hora que essa
// função É CHAMADA (clique do usuário) o resto do script já rodou, mas
// declarar como const aqui em cima quebraria (referência antes de existir).
let _infRelState = {};
function infOpenRelatar(){
  _infRelState = {};
  const types = {
    transito: {label:'Trânsito',     color:'#F97316', icon:FEED_TYPES.transito.icon, expMin:FEED_TYPES.transito.expMin},
    risco:    {label:'Alerta',       color:'#DC2626', icon:FEED_TYPES.risco.icon,    expMin:FEED_TYPES.risco.expMin},
    blitz:    {label:'Fiscalização', color:'#2563EB', icon:FEED_TYPES.blitz.icon,    expMin:FEED_TYPES.blitz.expMin}
  };
  _infRelState.types = types;
  document.getElementById('inf-relatar-title').textContent = 'Informe';
  document.getElementById('inf-relatar-back').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('inf-relatar-body').innerHTML = `<div class="qr-grid">` +
    Object.entries(types).map(([key,t]) =>
      `<div class="qr-btn" style="background:${t.color}" onclick="infRelatarSelType('${key}')">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="2">${t.icon}</svg>
        <span class="qr-label">${t.label}</span>
      </div>`
    ).join('') + `</div>`;
  const locTxt = document.getElementById('inf-relatar-loc-txt');
  locTxt.textContent = 'Localizando…';
  openMo('mo-inf-relatar');
  infRelatarBuscarLocalizacao();
}
// Pede o GPS e preenche o chip de endereço — separado do resto pra poder
// ser chamado de novo depois que a pessoa toca "Alterar" e volta.
function infRelatarBuscarLocalizacao(){
  const locTxt = document.getElementById('inf-relatar-loc-txt');
  if (!navigator.geolocation) { if (locTxt) locTxt.textContent = 'GPS indisponível'; return; }
  navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(async pos=>{
    _infRelState.lat = pos.coords.latitude; _infRelState.lng = pos.coords.longitude;
    try{
      const revR = await fetch(`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/reverse?lat=${pos.coords.latitude}&lon=${pos.coords.longitude}&zoom=16&format=json&accept-language=pt`,{headers:{'User-Agent':'MoB/1.0'}});
      const rev = await revR.json();
      _infRelState.address = rev.address?.road || rev.address?.suburb || rev.address?.neighbourhood || null;
    }catch(e){ _infRelState.address = null; }
    const el = document.getElementById('inf-relatar-loc-txt');
    if (el) el.textContent = _infRelState.address || 'Localização atual';
  }, ()=>{ const el=document.getElementById('inf-relatar-loc-txt'); if(el) el.textContent='Localização indisponível'; },
  {timeout:7000, maximumAge:60000});
}
// "Alterar" (16/08/2026, pedido do Yuri) — reaproveita a MESMA busca de
// rua próxima que "Minha área" já usa (infAbrirGps), só que em vez de
// abrir o painel de navegação, seta o endereço da corrida atual e volta
// pro Informe. Motorista pode não estar exatamente onde o GPS achou, ou
// o GPS errou — dá pra corrigir manualmente antes de publicar.
function infRelatarAlterarLocal(){
  closeMo('mo-inf-relatar');
  infAbrirGps('relatar');
}
function infRelatarPickLocal(nome){
  _infRelState.address = nome;
  infFecharOv('inf-ov-gps');
  openMo('mo-inf-relatar');
  const el = document.getElementById('inf-relatar-loc-txt');
  if (el) el.textContent = nome;
}
function infRelatarShowBack(label, fn){
  _infRelState.backFn = fn;
  const back = document.getElementById('inf-relatar-back');
  back.style.display = 'flex';
  document.getElementById('inf-relatar-back-lbl').textContent = label;
}
function infRelatarBack(){ if (_infRelState.backFn) _infRelState.backFn(); }

function infRelatarSelType(key){
  _infRelState.type = key;
  if (key === 'transito') return infRelatarShowTransito();
  if (key === 'blitz') return infRelatarShowPoliciamento();
  if (key === 'risco') return infRelatarShowAlertaStep1();
}

// Trânsito — barra 0 a 5, 1 toque publica.
functi
